# regulations

Regulations, orders and other official documents. Back to [the wiki](/index.md).

## document

- [Положение о порядке зачета результатов освоения обучающимися учебных предметов, курсов, дисциплин (модулей), практики](/regulations/credit-transfer-regulation.md): Regulates the procedure for recognizing academic results obtained at Innopolis University or other institutions.
- [Положение о порядке и основаниях перевода, отчисления и восстановления обучающихся](/regulations/transfer-expulsion-regulation.md): Regulates the procedures and grounds for transferring, expelling, and reinstating students at Innopolis University.
- [Положение о порядке и случаях перехода обучающихся с платного обучения на обучение за счет бюджетных ассигнований](/regulations/tuition-to-budget-transfer-regulation.md): Regulates the procedure for students at Innopolis University to transfer from paid education to budget-funded or university-funded study.
- [Положение о порядке проведения государственной итоговой аттестации (бакалавриат и магистратура)](/regulations/final-attestation-regulation.md): Regulates the state final certification procedures for bachelor and master students at Innopolis University.
- [Положение о предоставлении академического отпуска, отпуска по беременности и родам, отпуска по уходу за ребенком](/regulations/academic-leave-regulation.md): Regulates academic, maternity, and childcare leave procedures for students at Innopolis University.
- [Положение о формах, периодичности и порядке проведения текущего контроля успеваемости и промежуточной аттестации (бакалавриат и магистратура)](/regulations/attestation-regulation.md): This order approves the regulations for current academic performance monitoring and interim assessment for bachelor and master students at Innopolis University.
- [Положение по применению дисциплинарных взысканий за нарушения академической добропорядочности](/regulations/academic-integrity-regulation.md): This document establishes disciplinary procedures for academic integrity violations at Innopolis University.
- [Порядок оформления возникновения, приостановления и прекращения образовательных отношений](/regulations/enrollment-procedure-order.md): This document establishes the official procedure for initiating, suspending, and terminating educational relationships at Innopolis University.
- [Порядок проведения промежуточной аттестации с применением дистанционных образовательных технологий (бакалавриат и магистратура)](/regulations/remote-attestation-order.md): This document establishes the rules for conducting interim assessments using distance learning technologies for bachelor and master programs at Innopolis University.

## eduwiki

- [About this document](/regulations/about-this-document.md): Overview of the document summarizing the educational process at Innopolis University, including update procedures and annual version freezing.
- [Innopolis University Manifesto on Medium of Instruction](/regulations/innopolisuniversitymanifestoonmedium.md): Defines English as the primary medium of instruction for all academic activities while allowing other languages in informal or private contexts.
- [Overall Regulations](/regulations/overallregulations.md): A collection of links to key university documents including the medium of instruction manifesto, degree structures, and course lecturing guidelines.

## site/hotel

- [Договор оферты для физических лиц](/regulations/site-hotel-9a639135-dogovor-novyy-2024-fiz-litsa.md): This document is a standard offer agreement for accommodation services in the residential complex of Innopolis University.
- [Договор оферты для юридических лиц](/regulations/site-hotel-59898440-dogovor-novyy-2024-yur-litsa.md): This document outlines the terms for legal entities to book accommodation at the Innopolis University residential complex.
- [Дополнительные платные услуги](/regulations/site-hotel-df77c2d5-prikaz-na-dopolnitelnye-uslugi-v-zhk-1.md): This document lists the prices for additional paid cleaning and miscellaneous services available in the Innopolis University residential complex.
- [Инструкция по пожарной безопасности](/regulations/site-hotel-069b8f92-prikaz-instruktsiya-po-pb-202122.md): This document outlines fire safety rules for residents and visitors of the Innopolis University residential complex.
- [Информация об онлайн оплате](/regulations/site-hotel-8ec922f9-informatsiya-ob-oplate.md): Explains how to securely pay for accommodation services online via the Uniteller payment gateway.
- [КЛИНИНГ НОМЕРОВ](/regulations/site-hotel-92140743-komplektatsiya-nomerov.md): This document defines the schedule for replacing linens, providing amenities, and performing cleaning services in the university's residential complex.
- [ПИТЬЕВАЯ ВОДА](/regulations/site-hotel-b179ce35-pravila-polzovaniya-komnatoy-dlya-vody.md): Rules for using the drinking water storage room, including access, exchange procedures, and storage limits for residents.
- [ПРАВИЛА ПОЛЬЗОВАНИЯ ПРАЧЕЧНОЙ](/regulations/site-hotel-334b3894-pravila-polzovaniya-prachechnoy.md): Rules for using the laundry facility in a university dormitory, detailing equipment usage and safety guidelines.
- [Перечень документов для несовершеннолетних гостей (до 18 лет)](/regulations/site-hotel-d42a7c6a-formy-dokumentov-dlya-razmeshcheniya-v-z.md): List of documents required for minors under 18 to stay at Innopolis University residential complex.
- [Перечень документов для совершеннолетних гостей](/regulations/site-hotel-b6c5a551-formy-dokumentov-dlya-razmeshcheniya-v-z.md): List of documents required for adult guests to stay in the residential complex of Innopolis University.
- [Перечень и периодичность оказываемых услуг проживающим](/regulations/site-hotel-2f371091-prikaz-komplektatsiya-nomerov-period-ubo.md): This order establishes the list of consumables, soft furnishings, and cleaning service schedules for the university's residential complex.
- [Порядок взаимодействия администрации и комитета кампуса](/regulations/site-hotel-33cd4889-prikaz-ot-15-12-2022-od-ad0237.md): Regulates interaction between university housing staff and student residents in the campus complex.
- [Порядок доступа в номера Жилого комплекса](/regulations/site-hotel-857789fd-prilozhenie-pravila-dostupa-v-nomera.md): Regulates access to dormitory rooms by university administration and service staff for maintenance, inspections, and emergencies.
- [Порядок заселения, переселения и выселения](/regulations/site-hotel-3e7941b1-prikaz-poryadok-zaseleniya-i-vyseleniya.md): Regulates student housing procedures at Innopolis University.
- [Правила доступа в УЛК и ЖК](/regulations/site-hotel-edc4fcb0-pravila-dostupa-i-prebyvaniya-2022.md): This document establishes the access and stay regulations for the academic and residential buildings at Innopolis University.
- [Правила пользования велокомнатой](/regulations/site-hotel-9dd3d6d6-pravila-polzovaniya-komnatoy-dlya-velo.md): Rules for using the bicycle storage room at Innopolis University, outlining access, safety, and usage guidelines for students.
- [Правила пользования комнатой для воды](/regulations/site-hotel-37bc9559-pravila-polzovaniya-komnatoy-dlya-vody.md): Rules for using the drinking water exchange room in the university dormitory.
- [Правила пользования прачечными](/regulations/site-hotel-14b140fa-instruktsiya-po-polzovaniyu-prachechnymi.md): Rules for using the university laundry facilities, detailing operating hours, equipment usage, and safety guidelines for residents.
- [Правила пользования учебными комнатами](/regulations/site-hotel-2bd122f5-pravila-polzovaniya-uch-komnatoy.md): Rules for using study rooms at Innopolis University, covering access, conduct, and prohibited activities.
- [Правила проживания](/regulations/site-hotel-2b528e43-prilozhenie-k-prikazu-pravila-razmeshche.md): Regulates accommodation procedures and resident priorities for the Innopolis University residential complex.
- [Правила хранения багажа](/regulations/site-hotel-130f1551-pravila-vremennogo-khraneniya.md): This document establishes the rules and fees for storing students' personal belongings in the university dormitory during the summer break.
- [Приказ о перечне документов, необходимых для размещения в жилом комплексе АНО ВО "Университет Иннополис"](/regulations/site-hotel-da6a8ce7-dokumenty.md): This order approves the list and forms of documents required for accommodation in the Innopolis University residential complex.
- [СТОИМОСТЬ ПРОЖИВАНИЯ ДЛЯ СТУДЕНТОВ/СОТРУДНИКОВ (КОРПУСА 1-4)](/regulations/site-hotel-3ba1f830-raschet-stoimosti-razmeshcheniya-star-20.md): This document outlines the accommodation tariffs and discount rates for students and staff at Innopolis University housing.
- [СТОИМОСТЬ ПРОЖИВАНИЯ ДЛЯ СТУДЕНТОВ/СОТРУДНИКОВ (КОРПУСА 5-7)](/regulations/site-hotel-ac3dd05b-raschet-stoimosti-razmeshcheniya-nov2025.md): This document outlines the accommodation tariffs and discount rules for students and staff residing in University Innopolis dormitories.
- [Система штрафов](/regulations/site-hotel-bb11e2b9-opredelenie-stoimosti-tmts.md): This document outlines the fine schedule for violations of housing rules in the dormitory complex of Innopolis University.
- [Стоимость услуг размещения](/regulations/site-hotel-b6361abe-prikaz-o-stoimosti-2025-s-1-09-23.md): This document sets accommodation rates and rules for the Innopolis University residential complex starting September 2025.
- [Стоимость услуг размещения для льготных категорий](/regulations/site-hotel-4e80c68a-ob-utverzhdenii-izmeneniy-stoimosti-uslu.md): This document sets accommodation rates and discount policies for Innopolis University housing starting October 2025.

Единый федеральный список организаций, в том числе иностранных и международных организаций, признанных в соответствии с законодательством Российской Федерации террористическими](/regulations/site-university-5503fece-spisokorganizatsiy.md): This document lists organizations recognized as terrorist entities under Russian law, which universities must check to ensure compliance with national security regulations.

- [Polozhenie o reklamnoj rassylke](/regulations/site-university-f0f34598-polozhenie-o-reklamnoj-rassylke.md): Regulates the procedures for sending advertising, informational, and marketing messages by Innopolis University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_autonomous-nano-quadcopter]](/regulations/site-university-0e652667-nir-2022-autonomousnanoquadcopter.md): This document presents research on implementing collision avoidance for autonomous nano quadcopters at Ufa State Aviation Technical University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_biological-aging-mechanisms]](/regulations/site-university-dcbd334a-nir-2022-biologicalagingmechanisms.md): This article presents a statistical analysis of age-related changes in the neuromuscular system using physiological signals from volunteers at Kazan Federal University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_circular-cylinder]](/regulations/site-university-c6f421e8-nir-2022-circularcylinder.md): This document is a scientific article on fluid dynamics stability published by Southern Federal University researchers.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_computationaldesign]](/regulations/site-university-a6ad8886-nir-2022-computationaldesign.md): This article presents a computational design algorithm for synthesizing ergonomic exosuit spine modules using closed-chain linkages and genetic algorithms.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_dimension-reduction-techniques]](/regulations/site-university-8d9089f9-nir-2022-dimensionreductiontechniques.md): This article explores dimensionality reduction techniques for visualizing text datasets using self-organizing maps.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_eeg-changes]](/regulations/site-university-a21a5c6f-nir-2022-eegchanges.md): This document presents a scientific study on EEG patterns associated with COVID-19, authored by researchers from Moscow State University and other institutions.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_fusionofdata]](/regulations/site-university-55ae69a8-nir-2022-fusionofdata.md): This document is a research paper on sensor fusion for autonomous vehicles by Innopolis University researchers.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_gravity-compensator]](/regulations/site-university-778ae621-nir-2022-gravitycompensator.md): This article presents a gravity compensator concept for prismatic joints using linear springs and self-locking worm gears to achieve static balancing.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_grover-binary]](/regulations/site-university-cae11646-nir-2022-groverbinary.md): This document is a research article on quantum optimization algorithms published by Innopolis University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_localization-algorithms]](/regulations/site-university-e6d2e9b0-nir-2022-localizationalgorithms.md): This document presents a research article on mixed reality cross-device localization algorithms using point cloud registration.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_mpc-control]](/regulations/site-university-6aa061bd-nir-2022-mpccontrol.md): This document is a research article on bio-inspired control for quadruped robots published by ITMO University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_object-detection]](/regulations/site-university-3aa4121d-nir-2022-objectdetection.md): This document is a research article on object detection algorithms for robot navigation published by Bauman Moscow State Technical University.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_optimization-dictionary]](/regulations/site-university-a0d66e2c-nir-2022-optimizationdictionary.md): This document presents research on optimizing brain-computer interface dictionaries for silent speech recognition using statistical criteria.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_parallel-robot]](/regulations/site-university-6eadd33b-nir-2022-parallelrobot.md): This article presents a method for estimating position and orientation errors in large cable-driven parallel robots using force sensors.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_parametric-subresonance]](/regulations/site-university-b2d53b43-nir-2022-parametricsubresonance.md): This document presents a research article on the formal asymptotics of parametric subresonance in linear second-order differential equations.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_preliminarystudy]](/regulations/site-university-405efddc-nir-2022-preliminarystudy.md): This document is a research paper analyzing GitHub repository metrics to understand their impact on code quality and project popularity.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_robot-skill-transfer-learning]](/regulations/site-university-27212f05-nir-2022-robotskilltransferlearning.md): This document presents a research article on robot skill transfer learning using EMG-based force estimation.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_rotor-vibrations]](/regulations/site-university-58b3edea-nir-2022-rotorvibrations.md): This document is a research article on reducing rotor vibrations in active conical fluid film bearings using adaptive PI and DQN-based controllers.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_spherical-robot]](/regulations/site-university-bc322142-nir-2022-sphericalrobot.md): This document is a research article analyzing the dynamics and control of a spherical robot with an internal omni-platform.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_stiffness-analysis]](/regulations/site-university-2d460afa-nir-2022-stiffnessanalysis.md): This document presents a stiffness analysis of a double pantograph transmission system using virtual joint modeling and matrix structural analysis.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_tensegrity-structures]](/regulations/site-university-efb7022b-nir-2022-tensegritystructures.md): This research paper presents a state observer design method for tensegrity structures using non-minimal coordinates and orthogonal decomposition.
- [Read the article [NIR_2022_vbframework]](/regulations/site-university-9bcafc84-nir-2022-vbframework.md): This document is a research article on a vision-based framework for manipulating deformable linear objects using two robotic arms.
- [Rukovodstvo administratora PO SintezPlyus](/regulations/site-university-985f310d-rukovodstvo-administratora-po-sintezplyu.md): This document is an administrator's manual for the SynthesisPlus software complex used for data delivery and transformation.
- [Rukovodstvo polzovatelya SintezPlyus](/regulations/site-university-fcdfbca4-rukovodstvo-polzovatelya-sintezplyus.md): User manual for the SynthesisPlus service bus software at Innopolis University.

## sveden/common

- [Информация о наличии или об отсутствии государственной аккредитации образовательной деятельности по реализуемым образовательным программам, за исключением образовательных программ дошкольного образования, программ подгот — Копия выписки из государственной информационной системы «Реестр организаций, осуществляющих образовательную деятельность по имеющим государственную аккредитацию образовательным программам» [Реестровая_выписка]](/regulations/sveden-37991d3a-pdf.md): This document certifies the state accreditation status of educational programs at Innopolis University.
- [Копия выписки из реестра лицензий](/regulations/sveden-3ee95d5d-pdf.md): This document is an extract from the license registry confirming the current educational license status of Innopolis University.
